# Graph Report - qr code generator 6  (2026-08-04)

## Corpus Check
- 1560 files · ~2,704,400 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 23266 nodes · 30723 edges · 1777 communities (1483 shown, 294 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3166 edges (avg confidence: 0.78)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `df0110e8`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
